           │         def types(self):                                                                           │            
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                                                                  │            
           │         ```                                                                                        │            
           │         """                                                                                        │            
           │         pass                                                                                       │            
           │                                                                                                    │            
           │     def locals(self):                                                                              │            
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types.                                 │            
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit variable x                      │            
           │         and an integer variable y:                                                                 │
